downsample_camera(&camera_full, cfg.downsample_factor)\n  1437→    };\n  1438→\n  1439→    // Initialize Gaussians from visible points (using first view for now)\n  1440→    let cloud =\n  1441→ isotropic 3D sigma for consistent pixel-space footprint\n  1445→    let desired_sigma_px = 1.5f32;\n  1446→    let f_mean = 0.5 * (camera.fx + camera.fy).max(1.0);\n  1447→    for g in &mut gaussians #[cfg(feature = \"gpu\")]\n  1462→    let gpu_renderer = if cfg.use_gpu {\n  1463→        eprintln!(\"Initializing GPU renderer...\");\n  1464→        match GpuRenderer::new() {\n  1465→            Ok(r) => Some(r),\n  1466→            Err(e) => {\n  1467→                eprintln!(\"GPU renderer unavailable, falling back to CPU: {e}\");\n  1468→                None\n  1469→            }\n  1470→        }\n  1471→    } else {\n  1472→        None\n  1473→    };\n  1474→\n  1475→    #[cfg(not(feature = \"gpu\"))]\n  1476→    let gpu_renderer: Option<()> = None;\n  1477→\n  1478→    #[cfg(not(feature = \"gpu\"))]\n  1479→    if cfg.use_gpu {\n  1480→        return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\"GPU rendering requested but not compiled with --features gpu\"));\n  1481→    }\n  1482→\n  1483→    // Initialize background color (using first view's mean)\n  1484→    let first_target_linear = if let Some(v) = view_cache.get(&first_train_idx) {\n  1485→        v.target_linear.clone()\n  1486→    } else {\n  1487→        let first_target = load_target_image(&cfg.images_dir, &first_image_info.name)?;\n  1488→        let first_target_ds = downsample_image_smart(&first_target, };\n  1499→    let mut bg_opt = AdamVec3::new(cfg.lr_background, 0.9, 0.999, 1e-8);\n  1500→\n  1501→    // Optimizer state for SH coeffs (RGB × 16)\n  1502→    let mut sh_opt = [Vector3::<f32>::zeros(); 16];\n  1513→            for k in 0..16 {\n  1514→                out[k] = Vector3::new(g.sh_coeffs[k][0], g.sh_coeffs[k][1], g.sh_coeffs[k][2]);\n  1515→            }\n  1516→            out\n  1517→        })\n  1518→        .collect();\n  1519→    let mut opacity_logits: Vec<f32> = gaussians.iter().map(|g| g.opacity).collect();\n  1520→    let mut positions: Vec<nalgebra::UnitQuaternion<f32>> =\n  1523→        gaussians.iter().map(|g| g.rotation).collect();\n  1524→    let mut grad_accum_pos_norm: Vec<f32> = vec![0.0; gaussians.len()];\n  1525→    let mut function: GPU if available, otherwise CPU\n  1531→    #[cfg(feature = \"gpu\")]\n  1532→    let render = |gaussians: &[Gaussian], camera: &Camera, bg: &Vector3<f32>| {\n  1533→        if let Some(ref views\n  1552→    let initial_psnr = {\n  1553→        let mut psnr_sum = 0.0f32;\n  1554→        for &test_idx in test_indices_for_metrics {\n  1555→            let (test_camera, test_target_linear)    .ok_or_else(|| {\n  1563→                        anyhow::anyhow!(\"Camera {} not found\", test_image_info.camera_id)\n  1564→                    })?;\n  1565→                let test_rotation = as u32;\n  1578→                    downsample_image_smart(&test_target, target_width, target_height, cfg.downsample_factor)\n  1579→                };\n  1580→\n  1581→                let mut test_camera.width = test_target_ds.width();\n  1586→                    test_camera.height = test_target_ds.height();\n  1587→                    test_camera.fx *= scale_x;\n  1588→                    test_camera.fy *= scale_y;\n  1589→                    test_camera.cx *= scale_x;\n  1590→                    test_camera.cy *= scale_y;\n  1591→                }\n  1592→\n  1593→                let (test_indices_for_metrics.len() as f32)\n  1602→    };\n  1603→\n  1604→    eprintln!(\"Initial test PSNR: {:.2} dB\", initial_psnr);\n  1605→\n  1606→    // Initialize CSV logger if path provided\n  1607→    let mut csv_logger = if let Some(ref csv_path) = cfg.csv_output_path {\n  1608→        CsvLogger::new(csv_path).ok()\n  1609→    } else {\n  1610→        None\n  1611→    };\n  1612→\n  1613→    // Learning rate scheduling: exponential decay\n  1614→    // Decay all LRs by 10× over the full training (exponential)\n  1615→    let lr_decay_rate = 0.1f32.powf(1.0 / cfg.iters as f32);\n  1616→    let initial_lr_position = cfg.lr_position;\n  1617→    let initial_lr_rotation = cfg.lr_rotation;\n  1618→    let initial_lr_scale = cfg.lr_scale;\n  1619→    let initial_lr_opacity = // Track last densification stats for CSV logging\n  1630→    let mut last_densify_split: usize = 0;\n  1631→    let mut last_densify_clone: usize = 0;\n  1632→    let mut last_densify_prune: usize = // Cached coverage weights for GPU training\n  1638→    // Updated every COVERAGE_INTERVAL iterations at 4x downsampled resolution\n  1639→    #[cfg(feature = \"gpu\")]\n  1640→    const Option<Vec<f32>> = None;\n  1645→    #[cfg(feature = \"gpu\")]\n  1646→    let mut cached_coverage_dims: (u32, u32) = (0, 0);\n  1647→\n  1648→    for iter in 0..cfg.iters {\n  1649→        // Apply LR schedule: exponential decay\n  1650→        let lr_multiplier = lr_decay_rate.powi(iter as i32);\n  1651→        position_opt.lr = initial_lr_position * lr_multiplier;\n  1652→        rotation_opt.lr = initial_lr_rotation * lr_multiplier;\n  1653→        scale_opt.lr = initial_lr_scale * lr_multiplier;\n  1654→        opacity_opt.lr = initial_lr_opacity * lr_multiplier;\n  1655→        sh_opt.lr = initial_lr_sh * lr_multiplier;\n  1656→        bg_opt.lr = initial_lr_background * lr_multiplier;\n  1657→        let should_log =\n  1658→            cfg.log_interval > 0 && (iter == 0 || iter % cfg.log_interval == 0 || iter + 1 == cfg.iters);\n  1659→        let iter_start = if should_log { Some(Instant::now()) } else { None };\n  1660→\n  1661→        // Sample a random training view\n  1662→        let train_idx = *train_indices\n  1663→            .choose(&mut rng)\n  1664→            .expect(\"train_indices is non-empty\");\n  1665→        let (train_camera, train_target_linear) = if let Some(v) = view_cache.get(&train_idx) {\n  1666→            (v.camera.clone(), v.target_linear.clone())\n  1667→        } else {\n  1668→            let train_image_info = .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!(\"Camera {} not found\", train_image_info.camera_id))?;\n  1673→            let train_rotation = train_image_info.rotation.to_rotation_matrix().into_inner();\n  1674→ downsample_camera(&train_camera_full, cfg.downsample_factor);\n  1677→\n  1678→            let train_target = load_target_image(&cfg.images_dir, &train_image_info.name)?;\n  1679→\n  1680→            // Important: Downsample the image independently, then adjust camera to match actual image size\n  1681→            // This handles cases where COLMAP camera dimensions don't match actual image file dimensions\n  1682→            let train_target_ds = if (cfg.downsample_factor - 1.0).abs() < 0.001 {\n  1683→                train_target.clone()\n  1684→            } else {\n  1685→                // Determine target dimensions from actual image, not camera\n  1686→                let target_width = ((train_target.width() as f32) * cfg.downsample_factor).round().max(1.0) as u32;\n  1687→                let target_height = ((train_target.height() as f32) * cfg.downsample_factor).round().max(1.0) as u32;\n  1688→                downsample_image_smart(&train_target, target_width, target_height, 1693→            if train_camera.width != train_target_ds.width() || train_camera.height != train_target_ds.height() {\n  1694→                let scale_x = train_target_ds.width() as f32 / 1697→                train_camera.height = train_target_ds.height();\n  1698→                train_camera.fx *= scale_x;\n  1699→                train_camera.fy *= scale_y;\n  1700→                train_camera.cx *= scale_x;\n  1701→                train_camera.cy *= scale_y;\n  1702→            }\n  1703→\n  1704→            let train_target_linear = rgb8_to_linear_vec(&train_target_ds);\n  1705→            (train_camera, train_target_linear)\n  1706→        };\n  1707→\n  1708→        // Write params back into gaussians\n  1709→        for (i, g) in gaussians.iter_mut().enumerate() {\n  1710→            for k in 0..16 {\n  1711→                g.sh_coeffs[k][0] = sh_params[i][k].x;\n  1712→                g.sh_coeffs[k][1] = sh_params[i][k].y;\n  1713→                g.sh_coeffs[k][2] = sh_params[i][k].z;\n  1714→            }\n  1715→            g.opacity = opacity_logits[i].clamp(-10.0, 10.0);\n  1716→            g.position = positions[i];\n  1717→            g.scale = log_scales[i];\n  1718→            g.rotation = rotations[i];\n  1719→        }\n  1720→\n  1721→        // Coverage weighting (use current params)\n  1722→        if should_log {\n  1723→            eprintln!(\n  1724→                \"iter {}/{} start  view={}  gaussians={}  res={}x{}\",\n  1725→                iter + 1,\n  1726→                cfg.iters,\n  1727→                scene.images[train_idx].name,\n  1728→                gaussians.len(),\n  1729→                train_camera.width,\n  1730→                train_camera.height\n  1731→            );\n  1732→        }\n  1733→\n  1734→        let t0 = Instant::now();\n  1735→\n  1736→        // Coverage weighting: weight covered pixels more than background\n  1737→        // GPU path uses downsampled coverage computed periodically to reduce overhead\n  1738→        #[cfg(feature = \"gpu\")]\n  1739→        let weights: Vec<f32> = if gpu_renderer.is_some() {\n  1740→            // Update cached coverage 1755→            let coverage_bool = coverage_mask_bool(&gaussians, &train_camera);\n  1756→            coverage_bool\n  1757→                .iter()\n  1758→                .map(|&c| if c { 1.0 } else { 0.1 })\n  1759→                .collect()\n  1760→        };\n  1761→\n  1762→        #[cfg(not(feature = \"gpu\"))]\n  1763→        let weights: Vec<f32> = {\n  1764→            let              .collect()\n  1769→        };\n  1770→\n  1771→        // Forward and loss\n  1772→        let rendered_linear = render(&gaussians, &train_camera, &bg);\n  1773→        let (loss, &train_target_linear,\n  1777→                &weights,\n  1778→            ),\n  1779→            crate::optim::loss::LossKind::L1Dssim => l1_dssim_image_loss_and_grad_weighted(\n  1780→         ),\n  1786→        };\n  1787→        train_loss = loss; // Track most recent loss\n  1788→        let train_psnr = compute_psnr(&rendered_linear, &train_target_linear);\n  1789→        let (efficient for <10k Gaussians)\n  1797→                if let Some(reason) = &gpu_backward_disabled_reason {\n  1798→                    if iter == 0 {\n  1799→                        eprintln!(\"GPU backward disabled, using CPU backward: {reason}\");\n  1800→                    }\n  1801→                    render_full_color_grads(&gaussians, &train_camera, &d_image, &bg, disable_sh)\n  1802→                } else {\n  1803→                    match renderer.render_with_gradients(&gaussians, &train_camera, &bg, &d_image) {\n  1804→                    Ok((_pixels, grads_2d)) => {\n  1805→                    // TEMPORARY: CPU projection backward is default due to GPU shader bug\n  1806→                    // Use SUGAR_GPU_GRADIENTS=1 to enable GPU projection backward (experimental)\n  1807→                    let use_gpu_projection = std::env::var(\"SUGAR_GPU_GRADIENTS\").is_ok();\n  1808→\n  1809→                    let (d_pos, d_scales, d_rots, d_background) = if use_gpu_projection {\n  1810→                        // GPU projection backward (experimental - has bugs with full parameter training)\n  1811→                        let (d_positions, d_log_scales, d_rotations, _d_sh) =\n  1812→                            renderer.project_gradients_backward(&gaussians, &train_camera, &grads_2d);\n  1813→                        (d_positions, d_log_scales, d_rotations, grads_2d.d_background)\n  1814→                    } else {\n  1815→                        // CPU projection backward (default, stable)\n  1816→                        crate::gpu::chain_2d_to_3d_gradients_cpu(&grads_2d, &gaussians, &train_camera)\n  1817→                    };\n  1818→\n  1819→                    let dummy_img =                }\n  1822→                    Err(e) => {\n  1823→                        eprintln!(\"GPU backward failed, falling back to CPU: {e}\");\n  1824→    }\n  1828→                }\n  1829→            } else {\n  1830→                // CPU backward pass\n  1831→                render_full_color_grads(&gaussians, &train_camera, &d_image, &bg, disable_sh)\n  1832→            }\n  1833→\n  1834→            #[cfg(not(feature = \"gpu\"))]\n  1835→            render_full_color_grads(&gaussians, &train_camera, &d_image, &bg, disable_sh)\n  1836→        };\n  1837→        let t_backward = t1.elapsed();\n  1838→\n  1839→        // Convert dL/d(color) -> dL/d(SH coeffs) using per-Gaussian SH basis.\n  1840→        let mut d_sh:   }\n  1847→            }\n  1848→        } else {\n  1849→            // DC-only learning (k=0). Uses SH_C0 constant.\n  1850→            let sh0 = crate::core::SH_C0;\n  1851→            for i in 0..gaussians.len() {\n  1852→                d_sh[i][0] = d_color[i] * sh0;\n  1853→            }\n  let w = weights[i];\n        if w == 0.0 {\n            continue;\n        }\n        let diff = rendered[i] - target[i];\n        loss += w * diff.dot(&diff);\n        d[i] = diff * (2.0 * w / denom);\n    }\n\n    (loss / denom, d)\n}\n\nfn luminance(rgb: Vector3<f32>) -> f32 {\n    0.299 * rgb.x + 0.587 * rgb.y + 0.114 * rgb.z\n}\n\nfn d_luminance_to_rgb(dy: f32) -> Vector3<f32> {\n    Vector3::new(0.299 * dy, 0.587 * dy, 0.114 * dy)\n}\n\nfn gaussian_kernel_offsets(radius: i32, sigma: f32) -> Vec<(i32, i32, f32)> {\n    let mut out = Vec::new();\n    let denom = 2.0 * sigma * sigma;\n    for dy in -radius..=radius {\n        for dx in -radius..=radius {\n            let r2 = (dx * dx + dy * dy) as f32;\n            let w = (-r2 / denom).exp();\n            out.push((dx, dy, w));\n        }\n    }\n    out\n}\n\n/// Paper-style image loss: `0.8 * L1 + 0.2 * (1 - SSIM)`.\n///\n/// This is a deliberately direct, educational implementation:\n/// - SSIM is computed on luminance (linear RGB → Y).\n/// - Uses an 11×11 Gaussian window with sigma=1.5.\n/// - Returns per-pixel gradient dL/d(rendered_rgb) in linear RGB.\n///\n/// The loss is normalized by `sum(weights)` (like the weighted L2 loss).\npub fn l1_dssim_image_loss_and_grad_weighted(\n    rendered: &[Vector3<f32>],\n    target: &[Vector3<f32>],\n    weights: &[f32],\n    width: u32,\n    height: u32,\n) -> rendered[i] - target[i];\n        l1 += w * (diff.x.abs() + diff.y.abs() + diff.z.abs()) / 3.0;\n        let sign = Vector3::new(diff.x.signum(), diff.y.signum(), diff.z.signum());\n        d_l1[i] = sign * (w / (denom * 3.0));\n    }\n    l1 /= denom;\n\n    // SSIM on luminance.\n    let rendered_y: Vec<f32> = rendered.iter().copied().map(luminance).collect();\n    let target_y: Vec<f32> = target.iter().copied().map(luminance).collect();\n    let mut d_dssim_y = vec![0.0f32; rendered.len()];\n\n    let radius = 5i32; // 11x11\n    let sigma = 1.5f32;\n    let kernel = gaussian_kernel_offsets(radius, sigma);\n\n    let c1 = 0.01f32 * 0.01f32;\n    let c2 = 0.03f32 * 0.03f32;\n\n    let w_i = width as i32;\n    let h_i = height as i32;\n\n    let mut dssim = 0.0f32;\n    for py in 0..h_i {\n        for px in 0..w_i {\n            let center = (py * w_i + px) as usize;\n            let w_center = weights[center];\n            if w_center == 0.0 {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            // Normalize kernel weights for valid pixels at the boundary.\n            let mut wsum_local = 0.0f32;\n            for &(dx, dy, w) in &kernel {\n w) in &kernel {\n                let x = px + dx;\n                let y = py + dy;\n                if x < 0 || x >= w_i || y < 0 || y >= h_i {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                let wi = w / wsum_local;\n                let idx = (y * w_i + x) as usize;\n                mu_x += wi * rendered_y[idx];\n                mu_y += wi * target_y[idx];\n            }\n\n            let mut var_x = 0.0f32;\n            let mut var_y = 0.0f32;\n            let mut cov = 0.0f32;\n            for &(dx, dy, w) in &kernel {\n                let x = px + dx;\n                let y = py + dy;\n                if x < 0 || x >= w_i || y < 0 || y >= h_i {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                let wi = w / wsum_local;\n                let idx = (y * w_i + x) as usize;\n                let dxv = rendered_y[idx] - mu_x;\n                let dyv = target_y[idx] - mu_y;\n                var_x += wi * dxv * dxv;\n                var_y += wi * dyv * dyv;\n                cov += wi * dxv * dyv;\n            }\n\n            let a = 2.0 * mu_x * mu_y + c1;\n            let b = 2.0 * cov + c2;\n            let c = mu_x * mu_x + mu_y * mu_y + c1;\n new_log_scales = Vec::with_capacity(log_scales.len());\n    let mut new_rotations = Vec::with_capacity(rotations.len());\n    let mut new_grad_accum = Vec::with_capacity(grad_accum.len());\n\n    let mut kept = 0usize;\n    let mut pruned = 0usize;\n    let mut pruned_outliers = 0usize;\n    let mut pruned_needles = 0usize;\n    let mut split = 0usize;\n    let mut cloned = 0usize;\n    let mut cap_hit = false;\n    let mut kept_avg_grads: Vec<f32> = Vec::new();\n\n    // Max log-space anisotropy before pruning (ln(7.4) ≈ 2.0, so ~7:1 aspect ratio)\n    // Slightly more permissive than the per-step constraint (5:1) to allow some flexibility\n    const NEEDLE_PRUNE_ANISOTROPY: f32 = 2.0;\n\n    for i in 0..gaussians.len() {\n        // Prune outliers: gaussians too far from scene center\n        let distance_from_center = (positions[i] - scene_center).norm();\n        if distance_from_center > OUTLIER_DISTANCE_THRESHOLD {\n            pruned_outliers += 1;\n            pruned += 1;\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        let opacity = sigmoid(opacity_logits[i]);\n        if opacity < prune_opacity_threshold {\n            pruned += 1;\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Prune needles: Gaussians with extreme aspect ratios\n        let scale = &log_scales[i];\n        let max_s = scale.x.max(scale.y).max(scale.z);\n        let min_s = scale.x.min(scale.y).min(scale.z);\n        if max_s - min_s > NEEDLE_PRUNE_ANISOTROPY {\n            pruned_needles += 1;\n            pruned += 1;\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        let avg_grad = grad_accum[i] / (iters_in_window as f32);\n        kept_avg_grads.push(avg_grad);\n\n        // Always keep the original (but ensure it matches our parameter vectors).\n        let keep_idx = new_gaussians.len();\n        let mut keep = gaussians[i].clone();\n        for k in 0..16 {\n            keep.sh_coeffs[k][0] = sh_params[i][k].x;\n            keep.sh_coeffs[k][1] = sh_params[i][k].y;\n            keep.sh_coeffs[k][2] = sh_params[i][k].z;\n        }\n        keep.opacity = opacity_logits[i].clamp(-10.0, 10.0);\n        keep.position = positions[i];\n        keep.scale = log_scales[i];\n        keep.rotation = rotations[i];\n        new_gaussians.push(keep);\n        new_sh_params.push(sh_params[i]);\n        new_opacity_logits.push(opacity_logits[i]);\n        new_positions.push(positions[i]);\n        new_log_scales.push(log_scales[i]);\n        new_rotations.push(rotations[i]);\n        new_grad_accum.push(0.0);\n        kept += 1;\n\n        let can_add = new_gaussians.len() < cap;\n        if !(avg_grad > grad_threshold && can_add) {\n            if avg_grad > grad_threshold && !can_add {\n                cap_hit = true;\n            }\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        let sigma = mean_world_sigma(&gaussians[i]);\n        let jitter = (0.5 * sigma).clamp(1e-4, 5e-3);\n        let mut dir = Vector3::new(\n            rng.gen_range(-1.0f32..1.0f32),\n            rng.gen_range(-1.0f32..1.0f32),\n            rng.gen_range(-1.0f32..1.0f32),\n        );\n        if dir.norm_squared() < 1e-12 {\n            dir = Vector3::new(1.0, 0.0, 0.0);\n        } else {\n            dir = dir.normalize();\n        }\n        let new_pos = positions[i] + dir * jitter;\n\n        // We are turning 1 Gaussian into 2; split opacity between them so the render doesn't\n        // abruptly get more opaque just because we duplicated a primitive.\n        let child_opacity_logit = split_opacity_logit(opacity_logits[i], 2).clamp(-10.0, 10.0);\n        new_opacity_logits[keep_idx] = child_opacity_logit;\n        new_gaussians[keep_idx].opacity = child_opacity_logit;\n\n        if sigma > split_sigma_threshold {\n            // SPLIT: shrink BOTH parent and child by 0.2 in log-space (factor ~0.82)\n            // This prevents needles from persisting through splits\n            let scale_reduction = Vector3::new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2);\n            let shrunk_scale = log_scales[i] - scale_reduction;\n\n            // Shrink the parent (kept Gaussian) - CRITICAL FIX\n            new_log_scales[keep_idx] = shrunk_scale;\n            new_gaussians[keep_idx].scale = shrunk_scale;\n\n            // Create child with same shrunk scale, offset position\n            let mut g2 = gaussians[i].clone();\n            for k in 0..16 {\n                g2.sh_coeffs[k][0] = sh_params[i][k].x;\n                g2.sh_coeffs[k][1] = sh_params[i][k].y;\n                g2.sh_coeffs[k][2] = sh_params[i][k].z;\n            }\n            g2.opacity = child_opacity_logit;\n            g2.position = new_pos;\n            g2.scale = shrunk_scale;\n            g2.rotation = rotations[i];\n            new_gaussians.push(g2);\n            new_sh_params.push(sh_params[i]);\n            new_opacity_logits.push(child_opacity_logit);\n            new_positions.push(new_pos);\n            new_log_scales.push(shrunk_scale);\n            new_rotations.push(rotations[i]);\n            new_grad_accum.push(0.0);\n            split += 1;\n        } else {\n            // CLONE: same scale, slight offset.\n            let mut g2 = gaussians[i].clone();\n            for k in 0..16 {\n                g2.sh_coeffs[k][0] = sh_params[i][k].x;\n                g2.sh_coeffs[k][1] = sh_params[i][k].y;\n                g2.sh_coeffs[k][2] = sh_params[i][k].z;\n            }\n            g2.opacity = child_opacity_logit;\n            g2.position = new_pos;\n            g2.scale = log_scales[i];\n            g2.rotation = rotations[i];\n            new_gaussians.push(g2);\n            new_sh_params.push(sh_params[i]);\n            new_opacity_logits.push(child_opacity_logit);\n            new_positions.push(new_pos);\n            new_log_scales.push(log_scales[i]);\n            new_rotations.push(rotations[i]);\n            new_grad_accum.push(0.0);\n            cloned += 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    *gaussians = new_gaussians;\n    *sh_params = new_sh_params;\n    *opacity_logits = new_opacity_logits;\n    *positions = new_positions;\n    *log_scales = new_log_scales;\n    *rotations = new_rotations;\n    *grad_accum = new_grad_accum;\n\n    kept_avg_grads.sort_by(|a, b| a.total_cmp(b));\n    let grad_p50 = percentile(&kept_avg_grads, 0.50);\n    let grad_p90 = percentile(&kept_avg_grads, 0.90);\n\n    DensifyStats {\n        before,\n        after: gaussians.len(),\n        kept,\n        pruned,\n        pruned_outliers,\n        pruned_needles,\n        split,\n        cloned,\n        cap_hit,\n        grad_p50,\n        grad_p90,\n    }\n}\n\n/// M8: Train on multiple views with train/test split.\n///\n/// This extends M7 by:\n/// - Splitting images into train/test sets\n/// - Randomly sampling training views each iteration\n/// - Validating on held-out test views\n/// - Reporting PSNR metrics\npub fn train_multiview_color_only(\n    cfg: &MultiViewTrainConfig,\n) -> anyhow::Result<MultiViewTrainOutputs> {\n    // When disable_sh is true, force learn_sh to false (DC-only mode)\n    let learn_sh = if cfg.disable_sh {\n        if cfg.learn_sh {\n            eprintln!(\"Warning: --disable-sh forces learn_sh=false (DC-only mode)\");\n        }\n        false\n    } else {\n        cfg.learn_sh\n    };\n\n    let scene = load_colmap_scene(&cfg.sparse_dir)?;\n    if scene.cameras.is_empty() || scene.images.is_empty() {\n        return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\"Scene has no cameras/images\"));\n    }\n    if cfg.iters == 0 {\n        return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\"iters must be > 0\"));\n    }\n    if cfg.val_interval == 0 {\n        return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\"val_interval must be > 0\"));\n    }\n\n    let available_images = if cfg.max_images == 0 {\n        scene.images.len()\n    } else {\n        cfg.max_images.min(scene.images.len())\n    };\n    if available_images < 2 {\n Outliers: Distance > 50m from scene center\n2. Low opacity: < threshold (default 0.01)\n3. Needles: Anisotropy > 2.0 in log space (~7:1 aspect ratio)\n\n**Growth Criteria:**\n1. High gradient: avg_grad > threshold (default 0.0002)\n2. Under capacity: num_gaussians < max_gaussians\n3. Split vs Clone: Based on Gaussian size (sigma > split_sigma_threshold)\n\n**Key Issues Identified:**\n- Opacity splitting helps prevent sudden brightness changes\n- Scale reduction (0.2 log-space) during split prevents needle formation\n- Gradient accumulation drives densification decisions\n\nNow analyzing quality completion conditions...\n\n## Phase 3: Analyzing Recent Runs\n\n### Observations from runs/20251219_2055_onehour (onehour preset):\n- Iteration 1: PSNR=12.81 dB, 445 Gaussians (initial)\n- Iteration 101: PSNR=12.35 dB, 445 Gaussians (no densification yet)\n- Iteration 9801: PSNR=9.95 dB, 114,663 Gaussians\n- Iteration 10000: PSNR=5.42 dB, 114,663 Gaussians (FINAL)\n- **PROBLEM**: PSNR decreased from 12.81 to 5.42 dB - quality got WORSE!\n- Massive densification: 18,632 splits, 2,284 prunes\n- Background went negative: [-1.98, -2.03, -1.90] (unphysical!)\n\n### Observations from runs/20251219_0047_full (full preset):\n- Limited data shown (only iterations 1000, 2000, 3000)\n- PSNR also declining: 8.66 → 7.50 → 6.52 dB\n- Massive growth: 50,316 → 52,149 → 55,790 Gaussians\n- Background values seem more reasonable but very high: [0.95, 1.15, 0.84]\n\n### Critical Issues Identified:\n1. **PSNR degradation**: Quality is getting WORSE during training\n2. **Gaussian explosion**: Growing to 100K+ Gaussians without quality improvement\n3. **Background instability**: Negative/extreme values suggest optimization instability\n4. **Ineffective densification**: Adding Gaussians that don't improve (or harm) quality\n\nMoving to final phase: creating quality completion criteria...\n\n## Phase 4: Synthesis and Quality Criteria Creation\n\n### Existing Documentation Review:\nFound comprehensive QUALITY.md with detailed troubleshooting:\n- Common failure modes documented\n- Root causes identified (seed sensitivity, LR scaling, densification)\n- Solutions implemented and tested\n- Recommended configurations (20-75 images: stable, 100 images: recommended, 301 images: unstable)\n\n### Key Insights for Quality Criteria:\n1. **PSNR is the primary quality indicator** - should improve, not degrade\n2. **Gaussian health metrics** matter: anisotropy, opacity, gradients\n3. **Densification balance** is critical: too little = underfit, too much = explosion\n4. **Background stability** indicates optimizer health\n5. **Early detection** (iteration 1000-3000) prevents wasted compute\n\nCreating QUALITY_PROMPT.md based on comprehensive analysis...\n\n## Phase 5: Deep Dive into Densification Logic\n\n### Densification Implementation (trainer.rs:1031-1257)\n\n**Key Mechanisms Discovered:**\n\n1. **Pruning Conditions** (lines 1104-1126):\n   - **Outliers**: Distance > 50m from scene center → prune\n   - **Low Opacity**: sigmoid(opacity_logit) < threshold (default 0.01) → prune\n   - **Needles**: log-space anisotropy > 2.0 (~7:1 aspect ratio) → prune\n\n2. **Densification Trigger** (line 1153):\n   ```rust\n   if avg_grad > grad_threshold && can_add\n   ```\n   - avg_grad = accumulated gradient over window / window_iters\n   - Default grad_threshold: 0.0002\n   - Only densifies Gaussians with high gradient (not learning well)\n\n3. **Split vs Clone Decision** (line 1180):\n   - If `sigma > split_sigma_threshold`: **SPLIT** (shrink both children)\n   - If `sigma <= split_sigma_threshold`: **CLONE** (same size)\n   - sigma = mean world-space standard deviation of Gaussian\n\n4. **Split Behavior** (lines 1181-1208):\n   - Shrinks BOTH parent and child by 0.2 in log-space (~18% reduction)\n   - Prevents needle formation during splits\n   - Splits opacity between children to preserve brightness\n\n5. **Opacity Preservation** (line 1176):\n   ```rust\n   child_opacity_logit = split_opacity_logit(opacity_logits[i], 2)\n   // Solves: 1 - (1 - α_child)^2 = α_parent\n   ```\n   - Prevents sudden brightness increases when duplicating Gaussians\n\n**Critical Insight:**\nThe densification logic is **gradient-driven**, not quality-driven. It adds Gaussians where gradients are high (struggling to learn), but this doesn't guarantee quality improvement if:\n- The gradient threshold is too low → over-densification\n- The learning rate is too high → unstable gradients\n- The loss function is not capturing true visual quality\n\n### Problem Analysis\n\n**Why \"not enough good Gaussians\"?**\n\nFrom the code review, potential root causes:\n\n1. **Gradient Accumulation Window**:\n   - Gradients accumulated over `densify_interval` iterations\n   - If window is too short, may not identify truly problematic regions\n   - If window is too long, may miss rapid changes\n\n2. **Fixed Threshold Sensitivity**:\n   - `grad_threshold = 0.0002` is constant across all datasets\n   - Larger scenes may need different thresholds than smaller ones\n   - No adaptive thresholding based on median/percentile gradients\n\n3. **Cap Hit Behavior**:\n   - When `max_gaussians` is reached, no more densification\n   - Existing bad Gaussians stay, but no new good ones can be added\n   - Pruning criteria may be too lenient\n\n4. **Scale-Based Decision**:\n   - Split vs clone based on `sigma` (world-space size)\n   - May not correlate with {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929","id":"msg_01BdrvFsU3yYX94pbboiUuvB","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_012z4Z9VuQebt7iEQyzGx71f","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Volumes/My Shared Files/SplatRs/docs/QUALITY_PROMPT.md","content":"# 3DGS Training Quality Completion Conditions\n\n## Purpose\nThis document defines measurable completion conditions for high-quality 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) training runs. These criteria are derived from actual metrics recorded in `metrics.csv` and empirical observations of successful vs. failed training runs.\n\n## Metric Categories\n\n### 1. PRIMARY QUALITY METRICS\n\n#### 1.1 PSNR (Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio)\n**What it measures:** Reconstruction quality in decibels (dB). Higher is better.\n\n**Success Criteria:**\n- **Minimum (ACCEPTABLE):** PSNR improves by ≥0.5 dB from initial value OR stays within 0.5 dB\n- **Target (HIGH QUALITY):** PSNR improves by ≥1.5 dB from initial value\n- **Best Case:** Final PSNR ≥15 dB (dataset-dependent)\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- PSNR decreases by >1.0 dB from best observed value\n- PSNR peaks early (before iteration 1000) and degrades thereafter\n- Final PSNR < initial PSNR - 0.5 dB\n\n**Empirical Evidence:**\n- Failed run (20251219_2055_onehour): Initial 12.81 dB → Final 5.42 dB (-7.39 dB) ❌\n- Quality degradation starting at iteration 2000 correlates with gradient explosion\n\n#### 1.2 Training Loss\n**What it measures:** Mean squared error (L2) or L1+DSSIM loss. Lower is better.\n\n**Success Criteria:**\n- Loss converges (stabilizes or decreases) over final 1000 iterations\n- Loss does not increase monotonically for >500 consecutive iterations\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- Loss increases for >500 iterations consecutively\n- Loss diverges to >1.0 (indicates optimization failure)\n- Loss oscillates wildly (std dev > 0.05 over 100-iteration window)\n\n#### 1.3 Visual Quality (Subjective but Measurable Proxies)\n**Success Criteria:**\n- Rendered test views show recognizable scene structure\n- Model file size ≥200 KB (indicates sufficient Gaussian detail)\n- No visible artifacts (excessive needle-like Gaussians, missing regions)\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- Test renders are unrecognizable\n- Model file size <100 KB (too few/degenerate Gaussians)\n\n---\n\n### 2. GAUSSIAN POPULATION METRICS\n\n#### 2.1 Gaussian Count (num_gaussians)\n**What it measures:** Total number of active Gaussians in the scene.\n\n**Success Criteria (dataset-size dependent):**\n- **Small datasets (20-50 images):** 5,000 - 15,000 Gaussians\n- **Medium datasets (50-100 images):** 10,000 - 30,000 Gaussians\n- **Large datasets (100+ images):** 25,000 - 50,000 Gaussians\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- Excessive growth: >100x increase from initial count\n- Cap hit repeatedly: `max_gaussians` reached without quality improvement\n- Minimal growth: <2x increase (likely under-fitting)\n\n**Empirical Evidence:**\n- Failed run: 445 → 114,663 Gaussians (257x growth) with quality collapse ❌\n\n#### 2.2 Densification Activity\n**What it measures:** Split/clone/prune events over time.\n\n**Success Criteria:**\n- Densification activity (splits + clones) decreases over time\n- Pruning remains <10% of total Gaussians per densify_interval\n- Split/clone ratio remains roughly balanced (neither dominates completely)\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- Continuous high densification (>1000 splits/clones per interval) after iteration 5000\n- Pruning >25% of Gaussians per interval (indicates excessive turnover)\n- Zero densification early (before iteration 3000) with poor quality\n\n#### 2.3 Gaussian Health Metrics\n\n##### 2.3.1 Anisotropy (aniso_median, aniso_p90, aniso_max)\n**What it measures:** Aspect ratio of Gaussians (max_scale / min_scale).\n\n**Success Criteria:**\n- aniso_median: 1.0 - 3.0 (reasonable elongation)\n- aniso_p90: <5.0 (90% of Gaussians are not extreme needles)\n- aniso_max: <20.0 (no degenerate needles slip through)\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- aniso_median >5.0 (most Gaussians are needles)\n- aniso_p90 >10.0 (widespread needle formation)\n- aniso_max >100.0 (complete degeneration)\n\n##### 2.3.2 Opacity (opacity_median, opacity_low_pct)\n**What it measures:** Distribution of Gaussian opacities.\n\n**Success Criteria:**\n- opacity_median: 0.3 - 0.8 (Gaussians are contributing)\n- opacity_low_pct: <30% (most Gaussians are not wasted)\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- opacity_median <0.1 (Gaussians barely visible)\n- opacity_low_pct >50% (majority of Gaussians are ineffective)\n\n##### 2.3.3 Scale (scale_median)\n**What it measures:** Median world-space size of Gaussians.\n\n**Success Criteria:**\n- scale_median: 0.001 - 0.05 (scene-dependent, reasonable detail level)\n- Scale distribution is relatively stable (not collapsing or exploding)\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- scale_median <0.0001 (Gaussians too small, likely degenerate)\n- scale_median >0.5 (Gaussians too large, under-fitting)\n\n---\n\n### 3. OPTIMIZER STABILITY METRICS\n\n#### 3.1 Background Color (bg_r, bg_g, bg_b)\n**What it measures:** Learned background RGB values.\n\n**Success Criteria:**\n- **CRITICAL:** All background RGB values in range [0.0, 1.0]\n- Background values are relatively stable (change <0.05 per 1000 iterations)\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- **CRITICAL:** Any background value goes negative (unphysical, indicates divergence) ❌\n- Any background value >1.0 (also unphysical)\n- Background drifts rapidly (>0.2 change per 1000 iterations)\n\n**Empirical Evidence:**\n- Failed run: bg went from [0.27, 0.19, 0.14] → [-1.99, -2.03, -1.90] (MAJOR DIVERGENCE) ❌\n\n#### 3.2 Gradient Statistics (grad_p50, grad_p90)\n**What it measures:** Median and 90th percentile of position gradients.\n\n**Success Criteria:**\n- grad_p50: <1.0 (most Gaussians have converged)\n- grad_p90: <5.0 (high-gradient Gaussians are minority)\n- Gradients decrease over time (convergence)\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- **CRITICAL:** grad_p90 >20.0 (gradient explosion) ❌\n- grad_p50 increases over time (divergence)\n- Gradients oscillate wildly\n\n**Empirical Evidence:**\n- Failed run: grad_p90 jumped from 0.0005 → 34.0 at iteration 2000 (EXPLOSION) ❌\n\n#### 3.3 Gradient Health by Parameter Type\n**What it measures:** pos_grad_median, scale_grad_median, rot_grad_median\n\n**Success Criteria:**\n- All gradient medians decrease toward 0 over training\n- No single parameter type dominates (balanced learning)\n- Final gradient medians <0.01 (convergence)\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- Any gradient median >1.0 after 5000 iterations\n- Gradient medians increasing after 3000 iterations\n\n---\n\n## CHECKPOINT-BASED QUALITY GATES\n\n### Iteration 1000 (Early Validation)\n**Check:**\n- PSNR has not degraded by >0.5 dB from initial\n- Background values still in [0.0, 1.0]\n- Gaussian count: 500 - 3,000\n- grad_p90 <10.0\n\n**Action if failed:** Consider aborting (likely hyperparameter issue)\n\n### Iteration 3000 (Stability Check)\n**Check:**\n- PSNR ≥ initial PSNR (or within 0.3 dB)\n- Background values still in [0.0, 1.0]\n- Gaussian count growth is controlled (<10x from initial)\n- No gradient explosion (grad_p90 <15.0)\n\n**Action if failed:** Abort or reduce learning rates\n\n### Iteration 5000 (Densification Risk Window)\n**Check:**\n- PSNR trend is stable or improving\n- Gaussian count <50,000\n- Background values in [0.0, 1.0]\n- Densification slowing down\n\n**Action if failed:** Likely quality collapse; abort recommended\n\n### Final Iteration (Completion Validation)\n**Check:** All success criteria from sections 1-3\n\n---\n\n## EARLY ABORT CONDITIONS\n\n**Abort training immediately if ANY of these occur:**\n\n1. **Background Divergence:** Any bg RGB value goes negative or >2.0\n2. **Gradient Explosion:** grad_p90 >50.0\n3. **PSNR Collapse:** PSNR drops >3.0 dB from best observed value\n4. **Gaussian Explosion:** num_gaussians >200,000 (GPU memory risk)\n5. **Loss Divergence:** loss >1.5 or NaN\n6. **Numerical Instability:** Any NaN in metrics.csv\n7. **Zero Progress:** PSNR improvement <0.1 dB after 5000 iterations with quality still poor\n\n---\n\n## POST-TRAINING VALIDATION\n\n### 1. Model File Checks\n- model.gs file exists and is ≥100 KB\n- Model loads without errors in viewer\n- No NaN or Inf values in saved parameters\n\n### 2. Metrics CSV Checks\n- All rows are complete (no missing columns)\n- Final row shows expected iteration count\n- Trends are reasonable (no sudden discontinuities)\n\n### 3. Visual Validation\n- Test view renders match target images perceptually\n- No obvious missing geometry or artifacts\n- Scene is recognizable and detailed\n\n### 4. Viewer Validation (Interactive)\n- Model loads in real-time viewer\n- Scene is navigable from multiple viewpoints\n- No excessive needle artifacts when moving camera\n- Reasonable file size (<10 MB for most scenes)\n\n---\n\n## SUMMARY CHECKLIST\n\n### HIGH QUALITY RUN (All must pass):\n- [ ] Final PSNR ≥ Initial PSNR + 1.5 dB\n- [ ] Final PSNR ≥12 dB (absolute)\n- [ ] Background RGB always in [0.0, 1.0]\n- [ ] Gaussian count in healthy range (5K - 50K)\n- [ ] grad_p90 <5.0 at final iteration\n- [ ] opacity_low_pct <30%\n- [ ] aniso_p90 <5.0\n- [ ] Model file ≥200 KB\n- [ ] Test renders are high quality\n\n### ACCEPTABLE RUN (Minimum requirements):\n- [ ] Final PSNR ≥ Initial PSNR - 0.5 dB\n- [ ] Background RGB always in [0.0, 1.0]\n- [ ] No gradient explosion (grad_p90 never exceeded 20.0)\n- [ ] Gaussian count <100,000\n- [ ] Model file ≥100 KB\n- [ ] Scene is recognizable in test renders\n\n### FAILED RUN (Requires investigation/restart):\n- Any early abort condition triggered\n- Negative background values\n- PSNR degraded by >1.0 dB\n- Model file corrupt or <50 KB\n- Test renders unrecognizable\n\n---\n\n## AUTOMATED ASSESSMENT TEMPLATE\n\n```python\ndef assess_training_quality(metrics_csv_path):\n    \"\"\"\n    Assess training quality from metrics.csv\n    Returns: \"HIGH_QUALITY\" | \"ACCEPTABLE\" | \"FAILED\"\n    \"\"\"\n    import pandas as pd\n    df = pd.read_csv(metrics_csv_path)\n\n    # Extract key values\n    initial_psnr = df.iloc[0]['psnr']\n    final_psnr = df.iloc[-1]['psnr']\n    best_psnr = df['psnr'].max()\n    final_gaussians = df.iloc[-1]['num_gaussians']\n    final_bg = [df.iloc[-1]['bg_r'], df.iloc[-1]['bg_g'], df.iloc[-1]['bg_b']]\n    max_grad_p90 = df['grad_p90'].max()\n    final_grad_p90 = df.iloc[-1]['grad_p90']\n    final_opacity_low = df.iloc[-1]['opacity_low_pct']\n    final_aniso_p90 = df.iloc[-1]['aniso_p90']\n\n    # FAILURE CHECKS\n    if any(bg < 0.0 or bg > 1.0 for bg in final_bg):\n        return \"FAILED: Background divergence\"\n    if max_grad_p90 > 50.0:\n        return \"FAILED: Gradient explosion\"\n    if final_psnr < initial_psnr - 1.0:\n        return \"FAILED: PSNR collapse\"\n    if final_gaussians > 200000:\n        return \"FAILED: Gaussian explosion\"\n\n    # HIGH QUALITY CHECKS\n    if (final_psnr >= initial_psnr + 1.5 and\n        final_psnr >= 12.0 and\n        final_grad_p90 < 5.0 and\n        final_opacity_low < 30.0 and\n        final_aniso_p90 < 5.0 and\n        5000 <= final_gaussians <= 50000):\n        return \"HIGH_QUALITY\"\n\n    # ACCEPTABLE CHECKS\n    if (final_psnr >= initial_psnr - 0.5 and\n        max_grad_p90 < 20.0 and\n        final_gaussians < 100000):\n        return \"ACCEPTABLE\"\n\n    return \"FAILED: Did not meet minimum criteria\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## CONFIGURATION-SPECIFIC TARGETS\n\n### Micro Preset (20 images, 3000 iterations)\n- Expected PSNR gain: +1.5 to +3.0 dB\n- Expected Gaussians: 5,000 - Training Loss - CONVERGENCE INDICATOR\n    51→\n    52→**Success Criteria:**\n    53→- Loss decreasing over the first 50% of training\n    54→- Loss stabilizing or slowly decreasing in final 25% of training\n    55→- Final loss < 0.5 for L2 loss\n    56→- Final loss < 0.3 for L1+DSSIM loss\n    57→\n    58→**Failure Indicators:**\n    59→- ❌ Loss increasing for >1000 consecutive iterations\n    60→- ❌ Loss > 1.0 after iteration 5000\n    61→- ❌ Loss fluctuating wildly (variance > 0.2 over 100 iterations)\n    62→\n    63→**Measurement:**\n    64→- Track moving average over 100 iterations\n    65→- Compare iteration N to iteration N-1000\n    66→\n    67→---\n    68→\n    69→### 3. Visual Quality - PERCEPTUAL VALIDATION\n    70→\n    71→**Success Criteria:**\n    72→- Rendered test views show recognizable scene structure\n    73→- Major geometric features clearly visible\n    74→- Colors reasonably accurate (no solid color failures)\n    75→- Minimal black artifacts or floating splats\n    76→\n    77→**Failure Indicators:**\n    78→- ❌ Solid color renderings (file size <50 KB)\n    79→- ❌ Completely black or white images\n    80→- ❌ Unrecognizable scene (no correspondence to input views)\n    81→- ❌ Severe artifacts (large black regions, extreme noise)\n    82→\n    83→**File Size Heuristics:**\n    84→```\n    85→< 50 KB:     Solid color failure ❌\n    86→50-150 KB:   Very poor quality ⚠️\n    87→150-250 KB:  Marginal quality ⚠️\n    88→250-400 KB:  Good quality ✅\n    89→> 400 KB:    Excellent detail ✅\n    90→```\n    91→\n    92→**Validation:**\n    93→- Check rendered test views at iterations 1000, 3000, 5000, final\n    94→- Compare rendered vs target side-by-side\n    95→- Verify scene is recognizable by iteration 3000\n    96→\n    97→---\n    98→\n    99→## Gaussian Population Metrics\n   100→\n   101→### 4. Gaussian Count - CAPACITY MANAGEMENT\n   102→\n   103→**Success Criteria by Dataset Size:**\n   104→\n   105→**20-40 images:**\n   106→- Initial: 200-1,000 Gaussians\n   107→- Final: 5,000-15,000 Gaussians\n   108→- Growth ratio: 5-15×\n   109→\n   110→**50-75 images:**\n   111→- Initial: 400-2,000 Gaussians\n   112→- Final: 8,000-20,000 Gaussians\n   113→- Growth ratio: 4-10×\n   114→\n   115→**100 images:**\n   116→- Initial: 500-3,000 Gaussians\n   117→- Final: 15,000-50,000 Gaussians\n   118→- Growth ratio: 5-15×\n   119→\n   120→**Failure Indicators:**\n   121→- ❌ Zero growth (final == initial): Densification not working\n   122→- ❌ Explosive growth (>10× in <3000 iterations): Runaway densification\n   123→- ❌ Exceeding GPU capacity (>400K Gaussians on Metal GPU)\n   124→- ❌ >100K Gaussians for datasets <100 images\n   125→\n   126→**Healthy Growth Pattern:**\n   127→```\n   128→Iteration 0:     445 Gaussians (initial point cloud)\n   129→Iteration 500:   1,200 Gaussians (first densification)\n   130→Iteration 1000:  2,500 Gaussians (gradual growth)\n   131→Iteration 5000:  8,000 Gaussians (stabilizing)\n   132→Iteration 10000: 8,802 Gaussians (final) ✅\n   133→\n   134→vs.\n   135→\n   136→Iteration 0:     445 Gaussians\n   137→Iteration 5000:  73,548 Gaussians\n   138→Iteration 8000:  272,112 Gaussians\n   139→Iteration 8500:  423,821 Gaussians → GPU CRASH ❌\n   140→```\n   141→\n   142→---\n   143→\n   144→### 5. Densification Activity - GROWTH HEALTH\n   145→\n   146→**Success Criteria:**\n   147→- Split/clone events concentrated in first 60% of training\n   148→- Pruning rate 10-30% of split+clone rate\n   149→- Densification tapering off in final 30% of training\n   150→- grad_p90 staying between 0.0001 and 0.5\n   151→\n   152→**Failure Indicators:**\n   153→- ❌ Zero densification events (densify_split + densify_clone == 0)\n   154→- ❌ Excessive pruning (pruned > split + cloned)\n   155→- ❌ grad_p90 > 1.0 (gradient explosion)\n   156→- ❌ Densification cap hit repeatedly (cap_hit warnings)\n   157→\n   158→**Healthy Densification Pattern:**\n   159→```\n   160→Iterations 0-3000:    Heavy split/clone activity\n   161→Iterations 3000-7000: Moderate activity\n   162→Iterations 7000+:     Minimal activity (refinement phase)\n   163→\n   164→Example stats at iteration 5000:\n   165→densify_split: 1200\n   166→densify_clone: 800\n   167→densify_prune: 300  ✅ (15% prune rate)\n   168→grad_p90: 0.0521    ✅ (reasonable gradients)\n   169→```\n   170→\n   171→---\n   172→\n   173→### 6. Gaussian Health Metrics - QUALITY INDICATORS\n   174→\n   175→**Anisotropy (Aspect Ratio):**\n   176→- aniso_median: 1.5-4.0 (reasonable elongation)\n   177→- aniso_p90: 2.0-7.0 (some needles OK)\n   178→- aniso_max: <10.0 (no extreme needles)\n   179→\n   180→**Opacity Distribution:**\n   181→- opacity_median: 0.3-0.8 (useful range)\n   182→- opacity_low_pct: 10-40% (some weak Gaussians OK for blending)\n   183→\n   184→**Scale Distribution:**\n   185→- scale_median: 0.01-0.5 (scene-dependent, should be stable)\n   186→- scale_median should not drift >2× during final 50% of training\n   187→\n   188→**Gradient Magnitudes:**\n   189→- pos_grad_median: 0.00001-0.001 (decreasing over time)\n   190→- scale_grad_median: 0.00001-0.001\n   191→- rot_grad_median: 0.00001-0.001\n   192→\n   193→**Failure Indicators:**\n   194→- ❌ aniso_max > 20.0 (severe needle formation)\n   195→- ❌ opacity_median < 0.1 (all Gaussians too transparent)\n   196→- ❌ opacity_low_pct > 70% (most Gaussians ineffective)\n   197→- ❌ Gradient medians >0.01 after iteration 5000 (not converging)\n   198→\n   199→---\n   200→\n   201→## Optimizer Stability Metrics\n   202→\n   203→### 7. Background Color - OPTIMIZATION HEALTH\n   204→\n   205→**Success Criteria:**\n   206→- All RGB components in range [0.0, 1.0]\n   207→- Stable in final 30% of training (change < 0.1 per component)\n   208→- Background not pure black [0,0,0] or pure white [1,1,1]\n   209→\n   210→**Failure Indicators:**\n   211→- ❌ Any component < 0.0 or > 1.0 (unphysical, optimizer diverged)\n   212→- ❌ Drifting rapidly (>0.3 change per 1000 iterations)\n   213→- ❌ Extreme values even if in range (e.g., [0.001, 0.001, 0.001])\n   214→\n   215→**Example:**\n   216→```\n   217→Iteration 1000: bg=(0.46, 0.55, 0.47) ✅\n   218→Iteration 3000: bg=(0.48, 0.54, 0.46) ✅ (stable)\n   219→\n   220→vs.\n   221→\n   222→Iteration 1000: bg=(0.46, 0.55, 0.47) ✅\n   223→Iteration 2000: bg=(0.86, 1.00, 0.83) ⚠️ (approaching limit)\n   224→Iteration 3000: bg=(0.95, 1.15, 0.84) ❌ (GREEN > 1.0 - DIVERGED)\n   225→```\n   226→\n   227→---\n   228→\n   229→### 8. Gradient Statistics - CONVERGENCE HEALTH\n   230→\n   231→**Success Criteria:**\n   232→- grad_p50 decreasing over training\n   233→- grad_p90 staying <0.5 throughout\n   234→- Final grad_p50 <0.0001 (approaching convergence)\n   235→\n   236→**Failure Indicators:**\n   237→- ❌ grad_p50 increasing after iteration 3000\n   238→- ❌ grad_p90 >1.0 at any point (explosion)\n   239→- ❌ grad_p50 >0.001 at final iteration (not converged)\n   240→\n   241→**Healthy Gradient Evolution:**\n   242→```\n   243→Iteration 1000:  grad_p50=0.0005, grad_p90=0.05\n   244→Iteration 5000:  grad_p50=0.0002, grad_p90=0.02  ✅ (decreasing)\n   245→Iteration 10000: grad_p50=0.00005, grad_p90=0.005 ✅ (converged)\n   246→\n   247→vs.\n   248→\n   249→Iteration 5000:  grad_p50=0.0007, grad_p90=0.0007\n   250→Iteration 6000:  grad_p50=0.0023, grad_p90=0.2626\n   251→Iteration 7000:  grad_p50=0.0087, grad_p90=0.7824  ❌ (exploding)\n   252→```\n   253→\n   254→---\n   255→\n   256→## Checkpoint-Based Quality Gates\n   257→\n   258→### Iteration 1000 - Early Validation\n   259→\n   260→**PASS Requirements:**\n   261→- ✅ PSNR improved by ≥0.3 dB OR PSNR >11 dB\n   262→- ✅ Loss decreased from initial\n   263→- ✅ Background RGB all <1.0\n   264→- ✅ Gaussians 1.5× to 5× initial count\n   265→- ✅ Visual: Scene recognizable in rendered test view\n   266→\n   267→**ABORT if:**\n   268→- ❌ PSNR decreased >1.0 dB\n   269→- ❌ File size <50 KB (solid color)\n   270→- ❌ Background component >1.0\n   271→\n   272→---\n   273→\n   274→### Iteration 3000 - Stability Check\n   275→\n   276→**PASS Requirements:**\n   277→- ✅ PSNR improved by ≥0.8 dB from initial\n   278→- ✅ PSNR not decreasing (iteration 3000 ≥ iteration 2000)\n   279→- ✅ Gaussians 2× to 10× initial count\n   280→- ✅ grad_p90 <0.5\n   281→- ✅ Visual: Clear scene details visible\n   282→\n   283→**ABORT if:**\n   284→- ❌ PSNR degrading for 2+ consecutive checkpoints\n   285→- ❌ Gaussians >50K for <100 image datasets\n   286→- ❌ Any component of background >1.0\n   287→\n   288→---\n   289→\n   290→### Iteration 5000-8000 - Densification Risk Window\n   291→\n   292→**PASS Requirements:**\n   293→- ✅ Gaussian count growth rate slowing (<20% per 1000 iterations)\n   294→- ✅ grad_p90 stable or decreasing\n   295→- ✅ No GPU crashes or buffer overflows\n   296→- ✅ PSNR continuing to improve or stable\n   297→\n   298→**MONITOR:**\n   299→- Densification cap not being hit repeatedly\n   300→- File sizes staying >150 KB\n   301→- Loss not increasing\n   302→\n   303→---\n   304→\n   305→### Final Iteration - Completion Validation\n   306→\n   307→**HIGH QUALITY Requirements:**\n   308→- ✅ PSNR improvement ≥1.5 dB (dataset-dependent targets above)\n   309→- ✅ Final PSNR ≥12 dB\n   310→- ✅ Visual quality: Recognizable, detailed scene\n   311→- ✅ File size ≥200 KB\n   312→- ✅ Gaussian count in healthy range (see section 4)\n   313→- ✅ Background RGB all in [0.0, 1.0]\n   314→- ✅ grad_p50 <0.0001 Success Criteria\n   333→\n   334→### Micro Preset (20 images, 2000 iterations)\n   335→- **Target PSNR improvement:** +1.5 to +3.0 dB\n   336→- **Expected final Gaussians:** 8,000-12,000\n   337→- **Expected duration:** ~5-10 minutes\n   338→- **File size target:** 200-300 KB\n   339→\n   340→### Onehour Preset (75 images, 10K iterations)\n   341→- **Target PSNR improvement:** +2.5 to +5.0 dB\n Failure:** File size <50 KB at iteration 1000\n   359→2. **Background Divergence:** Any RGB component outside [0.0, 1.0]\n   360→3. **PSNR Collapse:** PSNR decreased >2.0 dB from peak\n   361→4. **Gradient Explosion:** grad_p90 >1.0\n   362→5. **Gaussian Explosion:** Growing >50% per 1000 iterations after iteration 3000\n   363→6. **GPU Crash:** Buffer overflow or memory errors\n   364→7. **Persistent Degradation:** PSNR decreasing for 3+ consecutive checkpoints\n   365→\n   366→**Rationale:** These conditions indicate fundamental optimization failure that will not recover. Aborting early saves compute time.\n   367→\n   368→---\n   369→\n   370→## Post-Training Validation\n   371→\n   372→After training completes, perform final validation:\n   373→\n   374→### 1. Novel views render correctly ✅\n   422→# - No major holes or artifacts ✅\n   423→# - Quality matches static renders ✅\n   424→```\n   425→\n   426→---\n   427→\n   428→## Summary Checklist\n   429→\n   430→Use this checklist to quickly determine training success:\n   431→\n   432→**✅ HIGH QUALITY Run:**\n   433→- [ ] PSNR improved ≥1.5 dB\n   434→- [ ] Final PSNR ≥12 dB\n   435→- [ ] Background RGB all in [0.0, 1.0]\n   436→- [ ] Gaussian count in healthy range\n   437→- [ ] File size ≥200 KB\n   438→- [ ] Scene recognizable with good detail\n   439→- [ ] No gradient explosions\n   440→- [ ] Model file saved successfully\n   441→\n   442→**⚠️ ACCEPTABLE Run (consider retraining):**\n   443→- [ ] PSNR improved 0.5-1.5 dB\n   444→- [ ] Some minor quality issues\n   445→- [ ] File size 100-200 KB\n   446→- [ ] Scene recognizable but artifacts present\n   447→\n   448→**❌ FAILED Run (must retrain):**\n   449→- [ ] PSNR decreased or <0.5 dB improvement\n   450→- [ ] Background RGB outside [0.0, 1.0]\n   451→- [ ] Solid color or unrecognizable rendering\n   452→- [ ] File size <100 KB\n   453→- [ ] Severe artifacts or crashes\n   454→\n   455→---\n   456→\n   457→## Automated Quality Assessment (Future)\n   458→\n   459→Recommended thresholds for automated pass/fail:\n   460→\n   461→```python\n   462→def assess_training_quality(metrics_csv, final_png):\n   463→    \"\"\"\n   464→    Returns: 'HIGH_QUALITY' | 'ACCEPTABLE' | 'FAILED'\n   465→    \"\"\"\n   466→    initial_psnr = metrics_csv.loc[0, 'psnr']\n   467→    final_psnr = metrics_csv.loc[-1, 'psnr']\n   468→    improvement = final_psnr - initial_psnr\n   469→\n   470→    bg_max = max(abs(metrics_csv.loc[-1, 'bg_r']),\n   471→                 abs(metrics_csv.loc[-1, 'bg_g']),\n   472→                 abs(metrics_csv.loc[-1, 'bg_b']))\n   473→\n   474→    file_size_kb = os.path.getsize(final_png) / 1024\n   475→\n   476→    # FAILED checks\n   477→    if improvement < 0.5:\n   478→        return 'FAILED'\n   479→    if bg_max > 1.0:\n   480→        return 'FAILED'\n   481→    if file_size_kb < 100:\n   482→        return 'FAILED'\n   483→    if final_psnr < 10:\n   484→        return 'FAILED'\n   485→\n   486→    # HIGH_QUALITY checks\n   487→    if improvement >= 1.5 and final_psnr >= 12 and file_size_kb >= 200:\n   488→        return 'HIGH_QUALITY'\n   489→\n   490→    # Otherwise ACCEPTABLE\n   491→    return 'ACCEPTABLE'\n   492→```\n   493→\n   494→---\n   495→\n   496→## References\n   497→\n   498→- **Metrics Definition:** src/optim/trainer.rs:43-132 (CsvLogger, GaussianStats)\n   499→- **Densification Logic:** src/optim/trainer.rs:1031-1257 (densify_and_prune)\n   500→- **Training Loop:** src/optim/trainer.rs:1266+ (train_multiview_color_only)\n   501→- **Known Issues:** docs/QUALITY.md (troubleshooting guide)\n   502→\n   503→---\n   504→\n   505→**Document Version:** 1.0\n   506→**Last Updated:** 2025-01-06\n   507→**Based on:** Analysis of metrics.csv output, QUALITY.md troubleshooting, and training loop implementation\n   508→\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. Use these criteria to determine when training has successfully completed versus when it has failed and should be aborted.\n\n---\n\n## Overall Success Definition\n\nA **successful high-quality 3DGS training run** produces a model that:\n1. Achieves measurable improvement in reconstruction quality (PSNR)\n2. Creates appropriate Gaussian coverage of the scene\n3. Maintains optimizer stability throughout training\n4. Generates visually accurate novel view renderings\n\n---\n\n## Primary Quality Metrics\n\n### 1. PSNR (Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio) - PRIMARY INDICATOR\n\n**Success Criteria:**\n- **Minimum improvement:** +1.0 dB from initial to final PSNR\n- **Target improvement by dataset size:**\n  - 20-40 images: +1.5 to +3.0 dB\n  - 50-75 images: +2.5 to +5.0 dB\n  - 100 images: +3.0 to +6.0 dB\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- ❌ PSNR degrading over time (final < initial)\n- ❌ PSNR flat for >3000 consecutive iterations\n- ❌ PSNR < 10 dB at any checkpoint after iteration 1000\n\n**Measurement Points:**\n- Initial: Iteration 1-100 (before densification)\n- Checkpoint: Every val_interval iterations\n- Final: Last iteration\n\n**Example Targets:**\n```\nInitial PSNR: 10.44 dB\nFinal PSNR:   14.51 dB  ✅ (+4.08 dB improvement)\n\nInitial PSNR: 12.81 dB\nFinal PSNR:   5.42 dB   ❌ (-7.39 dB degradation - FAILURE)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 2. Training Loss - CONVERGENCE INDICATOR\n\n**Success Criteria:**\n- Loss decreasing over the first 50% of training\n- Loss stabilizing or slowly decreasing in final 25% of training\n- Final loss < 0.5 for L2 loss\n- Final loss < 0.3 for L1+DSSIM loss\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- ❌ Loss increasing for >1000 consecutive iterations\n- ❌ Loss > 1.0 after iteration 5000\n- ❌ Loss fluctuating wildly (variance > 0.2 over 100 iterations)\n\n**Measurement:**\n- Track moving average over 100 iterations\n- Compare iteration N to iteration N-1000\n\n---\n\n### 3. Visual Quality - PERCEPTUAL VALIDATION\n\n**Success Criteria:**\n- Rendered test views show recognizable scene structure\n- Major geometric features clearly visible\n- Colors reasonably accurate (no solid color failures)\n- Minimal black artifacts or floating splats\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- ❌ Solid color renderings (file size <50 KB)\n- ❌ Completely black or white images\n- ❌ Unrecognizable scene (no correspondence to input views)\n- ❌ Severe artifacts (large black regions, extreme noise)\n\n**File Size Heuristics:**\n```\n< 50 KB:     Solid color failure ❌\n50-150 KB:   Very poor quality ⚠️\n150-250 KB:  Marginal quality ⚠️\n250-400 KB:  Good quality ✅\n> 400 KB:    Excellent detail ✅\n```\n\n**Validation:**\n- Check rendered test views at iterations 1000, 3000, 5000, final\n- Compare rendered vs target side-by-side\n- Verify scene is recognizable by iteration 3000\n\n---\n\n## Gaussian Population Metrics\n\n### 4. Gaussian Count - CAPACITY MANAGEMENT\n\n**Success Criteria by Dataset Size:**\n\n**20-40 images:**\n- Initial: 200-1,000 Gaussians\n- Final: 5,000-15,000 Gaussians\n- Growth ratio: 5-15×\n\n**50-75 images:**\n- Initial: 400-2,000 Gaussians\n- Final: 8,000-20,000 Gaussians\n- Growth ratio: 4-10×\n\n**100 images:**\n- Initial: 500-3,000 Gaussians\n- Final: 15,000-50,000 Gaussians\n- Growth ratio: 5-15×\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- ❌ Zero growth (final == initial): Densification not working\n- ❌ Explosive growth (>10× in <3000 iterations): Runaway densification\n- ❌ Exceeding GPU capacity (>400K Gaussians on Metal GPU)\n- ❌ >100K Gaussians for datasets <100 images\n\n**Healthy Growth Pattern:**\n```\nIteration 0:     445 Gaussians (initial point cloud)\nIteration 500:   1,200 Gaussians (first densification)\nIteration 1000:  2,500 Gaussians (gradual growth)\nIteration 5000:  8,000 Gaussians (stabilizing)\nIteration 10000: 8,802 Gaussians (final) ✅\n\nvs.\n\nIteration 0:     445 Gaussians\nIteration 5000:  73,548 Gaussians\nIteration 8000:  272,112 Gaussians\nIteration 8500:  423,821 Gaussians → GPU CRASH ❌\n```\n\n---\n\n### 5. Densification Activity - GROWTH HEALTH\n\n**Success Criteria:**\n- Split/clone events concentrated in first 60% of training\n- Pruning rate 10-30% of split+clone rate\n- Densification tapering off in final 30% of training\n- grad_p90 staying between 0.0001 and 0.5\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- ❌ Zero densification events (densify_split + densify_clone == 0)\n- ❌ Excessive pruning (pruned > split + cloned)\n- ❌ grad_p90 > 1.0 (gradient explosion)\n- ❌ Densification cap hit repeatedly (cap_hit warnings)\n\n**Healthy Densification Pattern:**\n```\nIterations 0-3000:    Heavy split/clone activity\nIterations 3000-7000: Moderate activity\nIterations 7000+:     Minimal activity (refinement phase)\n\nExample stats at iteration 5000:\ndensify_split: 1200\ndensify_clone: 800\ndensify_prune: 300  ✅ (15% prune rate)\ngrad_p90: 0.0521    ✅ (reasonable gradients)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 6. Gaussian Health Metrics - QUALITY INDICATORS\n\n**Anisotropy (Aspect Ratio):**\n- aniso_median: 1.5-4.0 (reasonable elongation)\n- aniso_p90: 2.0-7.0 (some needles OK)\n- aniso_max: <10.0 (no extreme needles)\n\n**Opacity Distribution:**\n- opacity_median: 0.3-0.8 (useful range)\n- opacity_low_pct: 10-40% (some weak Gaussians OK for blending)\n\n**Scale Distribution:**\n- scale_median: 0.01-0.5 (scene-dependent, should be stable)\n- scale_median should not drift >2× during final 50% of training\n\n**Gradient Magnitudes:**\n- pos_grad_median: 0.00001-0.001 (decreasing over time)\n- scale_grad_median: 0.00001-0.001\n- rot_grad_median: 0.00001-0.001\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- ❌ aniso_max > 20.0 (severe needle formation)\n- ❌ opacity_median < 0.1 (all Gaussians too transparent)\n- ❌ opacity_low_pct > 70% (most Gaussians ineffective)\n- ❌ Gradient medians >0.01 after iteration 5000 (not converging)\n\n---\n\n## Optimizer Stability Metrics\n\n### 7. Background Color - OPTIMIZATION HEALTH\n\n**Success Criteria:**\n- All RGB components in range [0.0, 1.0]\n- Stable in final 30% of training (change < 0.1 per component)\n- Background not pure black [0,0,0] or pure white [1,1,1]\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- ❌ Any component < 0.0 or > 1.0 (unphysical, optimizer diverged)\n- ❌ Drifting rapidly (>0.3 change per 1000 iterations)\n- ❌ Extreme values even if in range (e.g., [0.001, 0.001, 0.001])\n\n**Example:**\n```\nIteration 1000: bg=(0.46, 0.55, 0.47) ✅\nIteration 3000: bg=(0.48, 0.54, 0.46) ✅ (stable)\n\nvs.\n\nIteration 1000: bg=(0.46, 0.55, 0.47) ✅\nIteration 2000: bg=(0.86, 1.00, 0.83) ⚠️ (approaching limit)\nIteration 3000: bg=(0.95, 1.15, 0.84) ❌ (GREEN > 1.0 - DIVERGED)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 8. Gradient Statistics - CONVERGENCE HEALTH\n\n**Success Criteria:**\n- grad_p50 decreasing over training\n- grad_p90 staying <0.5 throughout\n- Final grad_p50 <0.0001 (approaching convergence)\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- ❌ grad_p50 increasing after iteration 3000\n- ❌ grad_p90 >1.0 at any point (explosion)\n- ❌ grad_p50 >0.001 at final iteration (not converged)\n\n**Healthy Gradient Evolution:**\n```\nIteration 1000:  grad_p50=0.0005, grad_p90=0.05\nIteration 5000:  grad_p50=0.0002, grad_p90=0.02  ✅ (decreasing)\nIteration 10000: grad_p50=0.00005, grad_p90=0.005 ✅ (converged)\n\nvs.\n\nIteration 5000:  grad_p50=0.0007, grad_p90=0.0007\nIteration 6000:  grad_p50=0.0023, grad_p90=0.2626\nIteration 7000:  grad_p50=0.0087, grad_p90=0.7824  ❌ (exploding)\n```\n\n---\n\n## Checkpoint-Based Quality Gates\n\n### Iteration 1000 - Early Validation\n\n**PASS Requirements:**\n- ✅ PSNR improved by ≥0.3 dB OR PSNR >11 dB\n- ✅ Loss decreased from initial\n- ✅ Background RGB all <1.0\n- ✅ Gaussians 1.5× to 5× initial count\n- ✅ Visual: Scene recognizable in rendered test view\n\n**ABORT if:**\n- ❌ PSNR decreased >1.0 dB\n- ❌ File size <50 KB (solid color)\n- ❌ Background component >1.0\n\n---\n\n### Iteration 3000 - Stability Check\n\n**PASS Requirements:**\n- ✅ PSNR improved by ≥0.8 dB from initial\n- ✅ PSNR not decreasing (iteration 3000 ≥ iteration 2000)\n- ✅ Gaussians 2× to 10× initial count\n- ✅ grad_p90 <0.5\n- ✅ Visual: Clear scene details visible\n\n**ABORT if:**\n- ❌ PSNR degrading for 2+ consecutive checkpoints\n- ❌ Gaussians >50K for <100 image datasets\n- ❌ Any component of background >1.0\n\n---\n\n### Iteration 5000-8000 - Densification Risk Window\n\n**PASS Requirements:**\n- ✅ Gaussian count growth rate slowing (<20% per 1000 iterations)\n- ✅ grad_p90 stable or decreasing\n- ✅ No GPU crashes or buffer overflows\n- ✅ PSNR continuing to improve or stable\n\n**MONITOR:**\n- Densification cap not being hit repeatedly\n- File sizes staying >150 KB\n- Loss not increasing\n\n---\n\n### Final Iteration - Completion Validation\n\n**HIGH QUALITY Requirements:**\n- ✅ PSNR improvement ≥1.5 dB (dataset-dependent targets above)\n- ✅ Final PSNR ≥12 dB\n- ✅ Visual quality: Recognizable, detailed scene\n- ✅ File size ≥200 KB\n- ✅ Gaussian count in healthy range (see section 4)\n- ✅ Background RGB all in [0.0, 1.0]\n- ✅ grad_p50 <0.0001 (converged)\n\n**ACCEPTABLE Quality (may require retraining):**\n- ⚠️ PSNR improvement +0.5 to +1.5 dB\n- ⚠️ Final PSNR 10-12 dB\n- ⚠️ File size 100-200 KB\n- ⚠️ Some minor artifacts but scene recognizable\n\n**FAILED Training:**\n- ❌ PSNR decreased or improved <0.5 dB\n- ❌ Final PSNR <10 dB\n- ❌ File size <100 KB\n- ❌ Solid color or unrecognizable rendering\n- ❌ Background RGB outside [0.0, 1.0]\n- ❌ Severe visual artifacts\n\n---\n\n## Configuration-Specific Success Criteria\n\n### Micro Preset (20 images, 2000 iterations)\n- **Target PSNR improvement:** +1.5 to +3.0 dB\n- **Expected final Gaussians:** 8,000-12,000\n- **Expected duration:** ~5-10 minutes\n- **File size target:** 200-300 KB\n\n### Onehour Preset (75 images, 10K iterations)\n- **Target PSNR improvement:** +2.5 to +5.0 dB\n- **Expected final Gaussians:** 8,000-15,000\n- **Expected duration:** ~60 minutes\n- **File size target:** 250-400 KB\n\n### Full Preset (100 images, 30K iterations)\n- **Target PSNR improvement:** +3.0 to +6.0 dB\n- **Expected final Gaussians:** 25,000-50,000\n- **Expected duration:** ~5-7 hours\n- **File size target:** 300-500 KB\n\n---\n\n## Early Abort Conditions\n\nTraining should be **aborted immediately** if ANY of these occur:\n\n1. **Solid Color Failure:** File size <50 KB at iteration 1000\n2. **Background Divergence:** Any RGB component outside [0.0, 1.0]\n3. **PSNR Collapse:** PSNR decreased >2.0 dB from peak\n4. **Gradient Explosion:** grad_p90 >1.0\n5. **Gaussian Explosion:** Growing >50% per 1000 iterations after iteration 3000\n6. **GPU Crash:** Buffer overflow or memory errors\n7. **Persistent Degradation:** PSNR decreasing for 3+ consecutive checkpoints\n\n**Rationale:** These conditions indicate fundamental optimization failure that will not recover. Aborting early saves compute time.\n\n---\n\n## Post-Training Validation\n\nAfter training completes, perform final validation:\n\n### 1. Model File Validation\n```bash\n# Check model was saved\nls -lh runs/YOUR_RUN/model.gs\n# Should be 1-10 MB depending on Gaussian count\n\n# Verify metadata\n# - num_gaussians matches final count\n# - training_psnr matches final PSNR\n# - bounds_min/max are reasonable (not NaN/Inf)\n```\n\n### 2. Metrics CSV Validation\n```bash\n# Check CSV has all iterations\nwc -l runs/YOUR_RUN/metrics.csv\n# Should match: (iters / log_interval) + 1 header\n\n# Verify no NaN values\ngrep -i nan runs/YOUR_RUN/metrics.csv\n# Should return nothing\n\n# Check PSNR trend\ntail -20 runs/YOUR_RUN/metrics.csv | awk -F, '{print $3}'\n# Should show stable or increasing values\n```\n\n### 3. Visual Validation\n```bash\n# Review final test view\nopen runs/YOUR_RUN/m8_test_view_rendered.png\nopen runs/YOUR_RUN/m8_test_view_target.png\n\n# Check for:\n# - Scene recognizable ✅\n# - Major features present ✅\n# - Reasonable colors ✅\n# - No massive artifacts ✅\n```\n\n### 4. Viewer Validation (if available)\n```bash\n# Load model in viewer\nsugar-viewer runs/YOUR_RUN/model.gs\n\n# Verify:\n# - Model loads without errors ✅\n# - Novel views render correctly ✅\n# - No major holes or artifacts ✅\n# - Quality matches static renders ✅\n```\n\n---\n\n## Summary Checklist\n\nUse this checklist to quickly determine training success:\n\n**✅ HIGH QUALITY Run:**\n- [ ] PSNR improved ≥1.5 dB\n- [ ] Final PSNR ≥12 dB\n- [ ] Background RGB all in [0.0, 1.0]\n- [ ] Gaussian count in healthy range\n- [ ] File size ≥200 KB\n- [ ] Scene recognizable with good detail\n- [ ] No gradient explosions\n- [ ] Model file saved successfully\n\n**⚠️ ACCEPTABLE Run (consider retraining):**\n- [ ] PSNR improved 0.5-1.5 dB\n- [ ] Some minor quality issues\n- [ ] File size 100-200 KB\n- [ ] Scene recognizable but artifacts present\n\n**❌ FAILED Run (must retrain):**\n- [ ] PSNR decreased or <0.5 dB improvement\n- [ ] Background RGB outside [0.0, 1.0]\n- [ ] Solid color or unrecognizable rendering\n- [ ] File size <100 KB\n- [ ] Severe artifacts or crashes\n\n---\n\n## Automated Quality Assessment (Future)\n\nRecommended thresholds for automated pass/fail:\n\n```python\ndef assess_training_quality(metrics_csv, final_png):\n    \"\"\"\n    Returns: 'HIGH_QUALITY' | 'ACCEPTABLE' | 'FAILED'\n    \"\"\"\n    initial_psnr = metrics_csv.loc[0, 'psnr']\n    final_psnr = metrics_csv.loc[-1, 'psnr']\n    improvement = final_psnr - initial_psnr\n\n    bg_max = max(abs(metrics_csv.loc[-1, 'bg_r']),\n                 abs(metrics_csv.loc[-1, 'bg_g']),\n                 abs(metrics_csv.loc[-1, 'bg_b']))\n\n    file_size_kb = os.path.getsize(final_png) / 1024\n\n    # FAILED checks\n    if improvement < 0.5:\n        return 'FAILED'\n    if bg_max > 1.0:\n        return 'FAILED'\n    if file_size_kb < 100:\n        return 'FAILED'\n    if final_psnr < 10:\n        return 'FAILED'\n\n    # HIGH_QUALITY checks\n    if improvement >= 1.5 and final_psnr >= 12 and file_size_kb >= 200:\n        return 'HIGH_QUALITY'\n\n    # Otherwise ACCEPTABLE\n    return 'ACCEPTABLE'\n```\n\n---\n\n## References\n\n- **Metrics Definition:** src/optim/trainer.rs:43-132 (CsvLogger, GaussianStats)\n- **Densification Logic:** src/optim/trainer.rs:1031-1257 (densify_and_prune)\n- **Training Loop:** src/optim/trainer.rs:1266+ (train_multiview_color_only)\n- **Known Issues:** docs/QUALITY.md (troubleshooting guide)\n\n---\n\n**Document Version:** 1.0\n**Last Updated:** 2025-01-06\n**Based on:** Analysis of metrics.csv output, QUALITY.md troubleshooting, and training loop implementation\n","numLines":508,"startLine":1,"totalLines":508}}}
